Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a supersymmetry algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 110. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 373/3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by supersymmetry generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 110, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 373. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on supersymmetry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 890. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 202 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 890 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the supersymmetry structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 202. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
